- **config-gc**: Garbage collection for your Nemo Code configuration. Periodically scans ~/.nemo (skills, memory, hooks, permissions, MCP servers, caches) for redundant, stale, orphaned, or low-value items, then walks the user through a confirm-each-deletion cleanup. Use when the user says "clean up my config", "config GC", "too many skills", "audit my setup", "my .nemo is bloated", or asks for a periodic config review.
